// ==== Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-IQ4_XS-none-maxctx-beellama.ps1 ====
# 144K ctx, kvarn4 K / kvarn3 V, tail 1024, b2048/ub256, think ON, no spec (beellama) — MAX CTX
#
# Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B (AtomicChat IQ4_XS) — MoE, 40L, 256 experts/8 active +1 shared,
# qwen35moe, 262K native ctx, vision-capable. On disk: 18.73 GB.
# MTP head ships SEPARATELY (not in this GGUF) — see the *-mtp-* scripts; this one is plain.
# MAX-CONTEXT think-ON copy. KV is reserved by -c only — think OFF vs ON does NOT change
# the reserved KV footprint at the same -c; think ON emits <think> into that window.
#   VRAM budget (kvarn4/kvarn3 @ ~27.5 kB/token):
#     128K = ~3.5 GB  -> 18.73 + 3.5  + 0.5 = 22.7 GB steady (default think-ON script)
#     144K = ~4.0 GB  -> 18.73 + 4.0  + 0.5 = 23.2 GB steady (this file)
#     160K = ~4.4 GB  -> 18.73 + 4.4  + 0.5 = 23.6 GB steady (spike likely OOMs)
#   -b 2048 (not 4096) keeps the prefill spike small enough to fit the ~0.8 GB margin
#   at 144K, mirroring AD-Q5_K-Q4_K maxctx which runs 144K/-b 2048 at 21.7 GB steady.
#   If prefill spikes OOM at 144K (desktop compositor/browser stealing VRAM), add
#   --n-cpu-moe 2-4 to offload MoE layers and regain ~1-2 GB — safe, minimal speed impact
#   (AGENTS.md: 0-4 safe). Do NOT restore -b 4096 without dropping ctx.
# think ON + no spec: --reasoning on, preserve_thinking:true. Sampling per AtomicChat card:
#   temp 0.6, top-p 0.95, top-k 20.
# kvarn4 K / kvarn3 V = house 128K+ pattern (pi-tune, ThinkingCap, AD-Q5_K-Q4_K maxctx).
# Source: beellama.cpp | https://huggingface.co/AtomicChat/Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-GGUF

. "$PSScriptRoot\beellama_common.ps1"

Write-Host "Launching: Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B IQ4_XS (144k MAX, think ON, beellama)" -ForegroundColor Green
& (Get-ServerBinary -Build "beellama") `
  -m $Model["Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-IQ4_XS"] `
  --port $Config.server.port --host $Config.server.host `
  -np 1 `
  --kv-unified `
  -ngl all `
  --ctx-size 147456 `
  -b 2048 -ub 256 `
  --cache-type-k kvarn4 --cache-type-v kvarn3 `
  --kv-tail-tokens 1024 `
  --flash-attn on `
  --cache-ram 0 `
  --jinja `
  --load-mode mlock `
  --no-warmup `
  --no-host --metrics `
  --log-timestamps --log-prefix --log-colors off `
  --reasoning on `
  --chat-template-kwargs '{"preserve_thinking":true}' `
  --temp 0.6 --top-p 0.95 --top-k 20 --min-p 0.0


// ==== Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-IQ4_XS-none-maxctx-ncmoe-beellama.ps1 ====
# 160K ctx, kvarn4 K / kvarn3 V, tail 1024, b2048/ub256, think ON, no spec, --n-cpu-moe 4 (beellama) — PUSHED CTX
#
# Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B (AtomicChat IQ4_XS) — MoE, 40L, 256 experts/8 active +1 shared,
# qwen35moe, 262K native ctx, vision-capable. On disk: 18.73 GB.
# MTP head ships SEPARATELY (not in this GGUF); this is plain (no spec).
# MAX-CONTEXT pushed to just under the 24 GB OOM cliff via --n-cpu-moe 4.
#   Explicit --n-cpu-moe (unlike the 144K maxctx file, which omits it and only adds
#   it if prefill spikes). Reason: at 160K the kvarn steady total is ~23.6 GB with
#   ~0.37 GB left for the prefill spike — that OOMs. Offloading 4 MoE layers frees
#   ~1.7 GB (experts ~0.43 GB/layer), dropping steady to ~21.9 GB -> ~2.1 GB spike
#   headroom, right under OOM but reliably launchable.
#   VRAM ladder (kvarn ~27.5 kB/token):
#     144K / ncmoe 0 : ~4.0 GB KV  -> 23.2 GB steady (old maxctx, tight ~0.8 GB)
#     160K / ncmoe 4 : ~4.4 GB KV  -> 21.9 GB steady (this file, ~2.1 GB spike room)
#     176K / ncmoe 4 : ~5.0 GB KV  -> 22.5 GB steady (borderline ~1.5 GB — next cliff)
#     192K / ncmoe 6 : ~5.4 GB KV  -> 22.0 GB (possible only if 8+ layers offloaded)
#   Do NOT remove --n-cpu-moe, restore -b 4096, or raise -c past 163840 without
#   re-checking resident VRAM (desktop compositor/browser steals ~0.5-1 GB).
# think ON + no spec: --reasoning on, preserve_thinking:true. Sampling per AtomicChat card:
#   temp 0.6, top-p 0.95, top-k 20.
# kvarn4 K / kvarn3 V = house 128K+ pattern. --n-cpu-moe 4 = safe, minimal speed impact
#   (AGENTS.md: 0-4 safe; beyond adds real decode penalty).
# Source: beellama.cpp | https://huggingface.co/AtomicChat/Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-GGUF

. "$PSScriptRoot\beellama_common.ps1"

Write-Host "Launching: Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B IQ4_XS (160k PUSHED-CTX, think ON, --n-cpu-moe 4, beellama)" -ForegroundColor Green
& (Get-ServerBinary -Build "beellama") `
  -m $Model["Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-IQ4_XS"] `
  --n-cpu-moe 4 `
  --port $Config.server.port --host $Config.server.host `
  -np 1 `
  --kv-unified `
  -ngl all `
  --ctx-size 163840 `
  -b 2048 -ub 256 `
  --cache-type-k kvarn4 --cache-type-v kvarn3 `
  --kv-tail-tokens 1024 `
  --flash-attn on `
  --cache-ram 0 `
  --jinja `
  --load-mode mlock `
  --no-warmup `
  --no-host --metrics `
  --log-timestamps --log-prefix --log-colors off `
  --reasoning on `
  --chat-template-kwargs '{"preserve_thinking":true}' `
  --temp 0.6 --top-p 0.95 --top-k 20 --min-p 0.0

// ==== Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-Q4_K_L-none-maxctx-beellama.ps1 ====
# 160K ctx, kvarn4 K / kvarn3 V, tail 1024, b2048/ub256, think ON, --n-cpu-moe 8 (beellama) — MAX CTX (balanced)
#
# Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B (Bartowski Q4_K_L) — MoE, 41 blocks, 256 experts/8 active +1 shared,
# qwen35moe, 262K native ctx, vision-capable. On disk: 20.70 GiB (22.24 GB decimal).
# Bartowski Q4_K_L ~0.49 GiB MoE per layer; --n-cpu-moe 8 frees ~3.9 GB, enough for
# 160K kvarn KV without pushing the 24 GB cliff while keeping most MoE on GPU for
# decode speed (AGENTS.md: 0-4 safe, 5-10 moderate penalty, 8 is mid-range).
#   VRAM ladder (kvarn4/kvarn3 @ ~27.5 kB/token):
#     80K / ncmoe 0 : ~2.1 GB KV  -> 23.3 GB steady (base file, no ncmoe)
#     131K / ncmoe 8 : ~3.4 GB KV -> 20.7 GB steady (safe, 3.3 GB spike room)
#     160K / ncmoe 8 : ~4.3 GB KV -> 21.6 GB steady (this file, ~2.4 GB spike room)
#     196K / ncmoe 8 : ~5.2 GB KV -> 22.4 GB steady (tight, needs more offload)
#     262K / ncmoe 41: ~6.9 GB KV ->  8.4 GB steady (full-offload file, MoE in RAM)
#   Do NOT remove --n-cpu-moe or raise -c without re-checking resident VRAM
#   (desktop compositor/browser steals ~0.5-1 GB). b2048 keeps prefill spike small.
# think ON + no spec: --reasoning on, preserve_thinking:true. Sampling per card:
#   temp 0.6, top-p 0.95, top-k 20. MTP present in GGUF but unused (none).
# kvarn4 K / kvarn3 V = house pattern; falls back to q4_0/q3_0 on unsupported layers.
# Source: beellama.cpp | https://huggingface.co/bartowski/Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-GGUF

. "$PSScriptRoot\beellama_common.ps1"

  --n-cpu-moe 8 `
  --port $Config.server.port --host $Config.server.host `
  -np 1 `
  --kv-unified `
  -ngl all `
  --ctx-size 163840 `
  -b 2048 -ub 256 `
  --cache-type-k kvarn4 --cache-type-v kvarn3 `
  --kv-tail-tokens 1024 `
  --flash-attn on `
  --cache-ram 0 `
  --jinja `
  --load-mode mlock `
  --no-warmup `
  --no-host --metrics `
  --log-timestamps --log-prefix --log-colors off `
  --reasoning on `
  --chat-template-kwargs '{"preserve_thinking":true}' `
  --temp 0.6 --top-p 0.95 --top-k 20 --min-p 0.0


// ==== Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-Q4_K_L-none-maxctx-fullmoe-beellama.ps1 ====
# 262K ctx, kvarn4 K / kvarn3 V, tail 1024, b2048/ub256, think ON, --n-cpu-moe 40 (beellama) — FULL MOE OFFLOAD / NATIVE MAX
#
# Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B (Bartowski Q4_K_L) — MoE, 41 blocks, 256 experts/8 active +1 shared,
# qwen35moe, 262K native ctx, vision-capable. On disk: 20.70 GiB (22.24 GB decimal).
# Full MoE-to-RAM offload: --n-cpu-moe 40 moves all 40 MoE layers (~19.5 GiB, ~0.49 GiB/layer)
# to system RAM, leaving only attention/SSM/norms (~1.0 GiB) on GPU. Decode is
# CPU-bound for MoE (heavy penalty per AGENTS.md: 25+ is extreme, 40 is max), but
# this is the only way Q4_K_L reaches its 262K native window on 24 GB VRAM.
#   VRAM budget (kvarn4/kvarn3 @ ~27.5 kB/token, 1.0 GiB non-MoE + 0.5 overhead):
#     131K : ~3.4 GB KV  ->  4.9 GB steady
#     196K : ~5.2 GB KV  ->  6.7 GB steady
#     262K : ~6.9 GB KV  ->  8.4 GB steady (this file, native max, ~15 GB headroom)
#   YaRN RoPE scaling (factor 4.0) could extend beyond 262K to ~1M, but native 262K
#   is the validated limit; use only if the task needs >262K and you accept extra
#   quality/speed tradeoff. Keep -b 2048 to limit prefill workspace.
# KVarN note: beellama.cpp supports kvarn2..kvarn8 on target ctx; --n-cpu-moe does
# not affect KVarN applicability. To trade quality for headroom, drop to
# kvarn3/kvarn2 rather than adding more offload. This file keeps kvarn4/kvarn3
# (house pattern) for quality at the cost of CPU MoE.
# think ON + no spec: --reasoning on, preserve_thinking:true. Sampling per card:
#   temp 0.6, top-p 0.95, top-k 20. MTP present in GGUF but unused (none).
# Source: beellama.cpp | https://huggingface.co/bartowski/Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-GGUF

. "$PSScriptRoot\beellama_common.ps1"

Write-Host "Launching: Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B Q4_K_L (262k FULL-MOE, think ON, --n-cpu-moe 40, beellama)" -ForegroundColor Green
& (Get-ServerBinary -Build "beellama") `
  -m $Model["Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-Q4_K_L"] `
  --n-cpu-moe 40 `
  --port $Config.server.port --host $Config.server.host `
  -np 1 `
  --kv-unified `
  -ngl all `
  --ctx-size 262144 `
  -b 2048 -ub 256 `
  --cache-type-k kvarn4 --cache-type-v kvarn3 `
  --kv-tail-tokens 1024 `
  --flash-attn on `
  --cache-ram 0 `
  --jinja `
  --load-mode mlock `
  --no-warmup `
  --no-host --metrics `
  --log-timestamps --log-prefix --log-colors off `
  --reasoning on `
  --chat-template-kwargs '{"preserve_thinking":true}' `
  --temp 0.6 --top-p 0.95 --top-k 20 --min-p 0.0


// ==== Qwen3.8-27B-AD-Q5_K-Q4_K-mtp-beellama.ps1 ====
# 128K ctx, kvarn4 K / kvarn3 V, tail 1024, b2048/ub256, think OFF, vision OFF, MTP (beellama)
#
# Qwen3.8-27B AD-Q5_K-Q4_K (Atomic Dynamic) — long window, think OFF.
# On disk: 17.27 GiB. Same mix as the 144K maxctx script; 128K is the comfortable
# floor with XL-class headroom:
#   17.27 + 3.52 + 0.5 = 21.3 GB steady. -b 2048 keeps the prefill spike under 24 GB.
#   Use this over AD-Q5_K when the prompt will exceed ~80K. Use maxctx for 144K.
# think OFF for all beellama Qwen3.8 scripts: instruct sample params
#   (temp 0.7, top_p 0.80, top_k 20, min_p 0.0, presence-penalty 1.5).
# MTP: --spec-type draft-mtp --spec-draft-n-max 2 (no draft GGUF).
# Source: beellama.cpp | https://huggingface.co/AtomicChat/Qwen3.8-27B-GGUF

. "$PSScriptRoot\beellama_common.ps1"

$modelKey = "Qwen3.8-27B-AD-Q5_K-Q4_K"
if (-not (Test-Path $Model[$modelKey]))
{
  Write-Error "Missing $modelKey at $($Model[$modelKey])"
  exit 1
}

Write-Host "Launching: Qwen3.8-27B AD-Q5_K-Q4_K + MTP beellama (128k, think OFF, vision OFF, kvarn KV)" -ForegroundColor Green
& (Get-ServerBinary -Build "beellama") `
  -m $Model[$modelKey] `
  --port $Config.server.port --host $Config.server.host `
  -np 1 `
  --kv-unified `
  --spec-type draft-mtp `
  --spec-draft-n-max 2 `
  -ngl all `
  --ctx-size 131072 `
  -b 2048 -ub 256 `
  --cache-type-k kvarn4 --cache-type-v kvarn3 `
  --kv-tail-tokens 1024 `
  --flash-attn on `
  --cache-ram 0 `
  --jinja `
  --load-mode mlock `
  --no-warmup `
  --no-host --metrics `
  --log-timestamps --log-prefix --log-colors off `
  --reasoning off `
  --chat-template-kwargs '{"preserve_thinking":false}' `
  --temp 0.7 --top-p 0.80 --top-k 20 --min-p 0.0 `
  --presence-penalty 1.5 --repeat-penalty 1.0


// ==== Qwen3.8-27B-AD-Q5_K-Q4_K-mtp-maxctx-beellama.ps1 ====
# 144K ctx, kvarn4 K / kvarn3 V, tail 1024, b2048/ub256, think OFF, vision OFF, MTP (beellama)
#
# Qwen3.8-27B AD-Q5_K-Q4_K (Atomic Dynamic) — high-context file on this box.
# On disk: 17.27 GiB (HF label 18.6 GB). Only +0.58 GiB vs UD-Q4_K_XL (16.69 GiB).
# House XL script is 160K kvarn at 21.6 GB steady so a 1.5-2 GB -b 4096 spike fits.
# This mix at 160K would be 22.2 GB steady — that spike OOMs on Windows/desktop.
# CONTEXT = 144K kvarn (highest that keeps XL-class headroom):
#   17.27 + 3.96 + 0.5 = 21.7 GB steady. -b 2048 (not 4096) cuts the prefill spike.
#   128K is comfortable; 160K is the first OOM cliff. Do NOT raise -c past 147456.
# think OFF + vision OFF: non-thinking sample params per Qwen3.8 card
#   (temp 0.7, top_p 0.80, top_k 20, min_p 0.0, presence-penalty 1.5).
# MTP: --spec-type draft-mtp --spec-draft-n-max 2 (no draft GGUF).
# Source: beellama.cpp | https://huggingface.co/AtomicChat/Qwen3.8-27B-GGUF

. "$PSScriptRoot\beellama_common.ps1"

$modelKey = "Qwen3.8-27B-AD-Q5_K-Q4_K"
if (-not (Test-Path $Model[$modelKey]))
{
  Write-Error "Missing $modelKey at $($Model[$modelKey])"
  exit 1
}

Write-Host "Launching: Qwen3.8-27B AD-Q5_K-Q4_K + MTP beellama (144k MAX, think OFF, vision OFF, kvarn KV)" -ForegroundColor Green
& (Get-ServerBinary -Build "beellama") `
  -m $Model[$modelKey] `
  --port $Config.server.port --host $Config.server.host `
  -np 1 `
  --kv-unified `
  --spec-type draft-mtp `
  --spec-draft-n-max 2 `
  -ngl all `
  --ctx-size 147456 `
  -b 2048 -ub 256 `
  --cache-type-k kvarn4 --cache-type-v kvarn3 `
  --kv-tail-tokens 1024 `
  --flash-attn on `
  --cache-ram 0 `
  --jinja `
  --load-mode mlock `
  --no-warmup `
  --no-host --metrics `
  --log-timestamps --log-prefix --log-colors off `
  --reasoning off `
  --chat-template-kwargs '{"preserve_thinking":false}' `
  --temp 0.7 --top-p 0.80 --top-k 20 --min-p 0.0 `
  --presence-penalty 1.5 --repeat-penalty 1.0


// ==== Qwen3.8-27B-AD-Q5_K-mtp-beellama.ps1 ====
# 96K ctx, kvarn4 K / kvarn3 V, tail 1024, b2048/ub256, think OFF, vision OFF, MTP (beellama)
#
# Qwen3.8-27B AD-Q5_K (Atomic Dynamic) — quality file on this box.
# On disk: 18.84 GiB (HF label 20.2 GB). Hybrid, 256K native, MTP head in-file.
# This is the highest-fidelity Atomic file that still leaves a usable window on the
# 24 GB 3090/250W. It is NOT a 128K card:
#   18.84 model + 128K kvarn (~3.5 GB, scaled from UD-Q4_K_XL 160K=4.4 GB) + 0.5 oh
#   = 22.8 GB steady, then the -b 4096 prefill spike OOMs (same spike that capped
#   UD-Q4_K_XL at 160K / 21.6 GB steady).
# CONTEXT = 96K kvarn. 18.84 + 2.64 + 0.5 = 22.0 GB steady, ~2 GB for prefill.
# Cache is kvarn4/kvarn3 (house turbo path). q8_0 at a useful window does not fit
# this quant — 64K q8 lands near the same VRAM as 96K kvarn and loses the extra
# tokens. Do NOT raise -c past 98304 or restore -b 4096 without dropping ctx.
# think OFF for all beellama Qwen3.8 scripts: instruct sample params
#   (temp 0.7, top_p 0.80, top_k 20, min_p 0.0, presence-penalty 1.5).
# MTP: --spec-type draft-mtp --spec-draft-n-max 2 (no draft GGUF).
# Source: beellama.cpp | https://huggingface.co/AtomicChat/Qwen3.8-27B-GGUF

. "$PSScriptRoot\beellama_common.ps1"

$modelKey = "Qwen3.8-27B-AD-Q5_K"

{

Write-Host "Launching: Qwen3.8-27B AD-Q5_K + MTP beellama (96k, think OFF, vision OFF, kvarn KV)" -ForegroundColor Green
& (Get-ServerBinary -Build "beellama") `
  -m $Model[$modelKey] `
  --port $Config.server.port --host $Config.server.host `
  -np 1 `
  --kv-unified `
  --spec-type draft-mtp `
  --spec-draft-n-max 2 `
  -ngl all `
  --ctx-size 98304 `
  -b 2048 -ub 256 `
  --cache-type-k kvarn4 --cache-type-v kvarn3 `
  --kv-tail-tokens 1024 `
  --flash-attn on `
  --cache-ram 0 `
  --jinja `
  --load-mode mlock `
  --no-warmup `
  --no-host --metrics `
  --log-timestamps --log-prefix --log-colors off `
  --reasoning off `

// ==== Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q4_K_M-dspark-beellama.ps1 ====
# 64K ctx, q4_0 KV, tail 1024, b4096/ub512, think ON, DSPark spec-dec (beellama, draft-dspark)
#
# Qwen3.8-27B UD-Q4_K_M (Unsloth Dynamic V3) + DimInfer Qwen3.8-27B-DSpark-Q8_0 drafter.
# DSPark = DeepSeek-V4-style cross-attention drafter head (5L, block 15, markov/conf heads).
#   Per DimInfer card: trained on Q4_K_M hidden states, transfers to other quants (UD-Q4_K_M here).
#   2.0 GB Q8_0 draft; --spec-draft-model <DSPark> is a SEPARATE file (not the in-GGUF MTP head).
# Launch recipe (card): -m target -md drafter --spec-type draft-dspark --spec-draft-n-max 4 -ngl 99 -ngld 99
#   --spec-draft-n-max 4 is the measured optimum (peaks 101 tok/s vs 99/97/92 at n=3/5/6; card table).
#   --spec-draft-ngl all is required — draft left on CPU erases the speedup (card: "both are needed").
# VRAM on 24 GB 3090/250W: 15.3 model + 2.0 draft + ~2.8 q4_0 KV @ 64K + 0.5 ≈ 20.6 GB — comfortable.
#   Do NOT raise to 128K q4_0 (~+2.2 GB KV) without dropping the draft offload or adding --n-cpu-moe;
#   DSPark draft + 128K would land ~23.6 GB resident, no headroom for prefill spike.
# think ON (low) + vision OFF: --reasoning on, reasoning_effort low via chat-template-kwargs.
#   Low keeps thinking brief; kwargs preserve_thinking:true + reasoning_effort:low.
#   Sample params per unsloth card (temp 0.7, top-p 0.80, top-k 20, min-p 0.0, presence-penalty 1.5).
# Source: beellama.cpp | https://huggingface.co/DimInfer/Qwen3.8-27B-Dspark-v1
#         | https://github.com/Anbeeld/beellama.cpp/issues/128 (comment 5312029586)

. "$PSScriptRoot\beellama_common.ps1"

$modelKey = "Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q4_K_M"
if (-not (Test-Path $Model[$modelKey]))
{
  Write-Error "Missing $modelKey at $($Model[$modelKey])"
  exit 1
}
if (-not (Test-Path $Drafter["Qwen3.8-DSpark"]))
{
  Write-Error "Missing DSPark drafter at $($Drafter['Qwen3.8-DSpark']) — expected D:\.lmstudio\models\dimInfer\Qwen3.8-27B-DSpark-Q8_0.gguf"
  exit 1
}

Write-Host "Launching: Qwen3.8-27B UD-Q4_K_M (Dynamic V3) + DSPark drafter, beellama (64k, think ON, vision OFF)" -ForegroundColor Green
& (Get-ServerBinary -Build "beellama") `
  -m $Model[$modelKey] `
  --spec-draft-model $Drafter["Qwen3.8-DSpark"] `
  --spec-type draft-dspark `
  --spec-draft-n-max 4 `
  --spec-draft-ngl all `
  --port $Config.server.port --host $Config.server.host `
  -np 1 `
  --kv-unified `
  -ngl all `
  --ctx-size 65536 `
  -b 4096 -ub 512 `
  --cache-type-k q4_0 --cache-type-v q4_0 `
  --kv-tail-tokens 1024 `
  --flash-attn on `
  --cache-ram 0 `
  --jinja `
  --load-mode mlock `
  --no-warmup `
  --no-host --metrics `
  --log-timestamps --log-prefix --log-colors off `
  --reasoning on `
  --chat-template-kwargs '{"preserve_thinking":true, "reasoning_effort":"low"}' `
  --temp 0.7 --top-p 0.80 --top-k 20 --min-p 0.0 `
  --presence-penalty 1.5 --repeat-penalty 1.0

// ==== Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q4_K_M-mtp-quality-beellama.ps1 ====
# 64K ctx, q8_0 K / q8_0 V, b2048/ub256, think OFF, vision OFF, MTP (beellama)
#
# Qwen3.8-27B UD-Q4_K_M (Unsloth Dynamic V3) — PURE QUALITY copy of the 160K maxctx script.
# V3.0 quant, 15.3 GiB. This one spends the VRAM on cache precision, not window.
#   Maxctx:   160K kvarn4/kvarn3, -b 4096, ~20.2 GB steady (max-ctx / speed).
#   This file: 64K q8_0/q8_0. Same ~20.2 GB class — q8 at 64K ≈ kvarn at 160K.
#   V3 M is ~1.4 GiB smaller than the removed UD-Q4_K_XL, so there is headroom; keep
#   64K q8 + -b 2048 conservative (no mmproj — 0.87 GiB would force a ctx or cache cut).
# WHY 64K (not 128K/160K): this is a QUALITY script — every token of context buys
#   less cache fidelity on a fixed 24 GB budget. q8_0 KV is ~4x the bytes/token of
#   kvarn, so a full 128K window would push past the VRAM ceiling and force dropping
#   to kvarn — which is precisely the precision cut this file exists to avoid.
#   64K q8_0 costs the same VRAM as 160K kvarn (maxctx script) but keeps the highest
#   cache quality; 64K still covers long code files and reasoning traces comfortably.
#   The 128K (kvarn) and 160K (kvarn) siblings are there when you need the window.
# Hybrid Qwen3.8 is KV-sensitive; q8 is the quality floor. kvarn/q4 stay on the maxctx script.
# think OFF + vision OFF: --reasoning off, non-thinking sample params
#   per unsloth card (temp 0.7, top-p 0.80, top-k 20, min-p 0.0,
#   presence-penalty 1.5, repetition-penalty 1.0).
# MTP head verified baked in (blk.*.nextn.* tensors): --spec-type draft-mtp --spec-draft-n-max 2.
# Sources: https://unsloth.ai/docs/models/qwen3.8 | https://github.com/sudoingX/qwen38-mtp

. "$PSScriptRoot\beellama_common.ps1"

Write-Host "Launching: Qwen3.8-27B UD-Q4_K_M (Dynamic V3) + MTP beellama (64k QUALITY, think OFF, vision OFF, q8 KV)" -ForegroundColor Green
& (Get-ServerBinary -Build "beellama") `
  -m $Model["Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q4_K_M"] `
  --port $Config.server.port --host $Config.server.host `
  -np 1 `
  --kv-unified `
  --spec-type draft-mtp `
  --spec-draft-n-max 2 `
  -ngl all `
  --ctx-size 65536 `
  -b 2048 -ub 256 `
  --cache-type-k q8_0 --cache-type-v q8_0 `
  --flash-attn on `
  --cache-ram 0 `
  --jinja `
  --load-mode mlock `
  --no-warmup `
  --no-host --metrics `
  --log-timestamps --log-prefix --log-colors off `
  --reasoning off `
  --chat-template-kwargs '{"preserve_thinking":false}' `
  --temp 0.7 --top-p 0.80 --top-k 20 --min-p 0.0 `
  --presence-penalty 1.5 --repeat-penalty 1.0

  --kv-tail-tokens 1024 `

  --jinja `
  --load-mode mlock `
  --no-warmup `
  --no-host --metrics `
  --log-timestamps --log-prefix --log-colors off `
  --reasoning off `
  --chat-template-kwargs '{"preserve_thinking":false}' `

  --presence-penalty 1.5 --repeat-penalty 1.0

// ==== Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q5_K_S-mtp-maxctx-think-beellama.ps1 ====
# 144K ctx, kvarn4 K / kvarn3 V, tail 1024, b2048/ub256, think ON, vision OFF, MTP (beellama) — MAX CTX
#
# Qwen3.8-27B UD-Q5_K_S (Unsloth Dynamic) — replacement for the AtomicChat AD-Q5_K-Q4_K set.
# On disk: 17.38 GiB — only +0.11 GiB vs AD-Q5_K-Q4_K (17.27), so the proven AD maxctx
# budget carries over:
#   17.38 + 3.96 + 0.5 = 21.8 GB steady at 144K kvarn. -b 2048 (not 4096) cuts the prefill
#   spike; 128K is comfortable, 160K is the first OOM cliff. Do NOT raise -c past 147456.
# MTP head verified baked in (nextn tensors in GGUF header): --spec-type draft-mtp.
# think ON (low) + vision OFF: --reasoning on, preserve_thinking:true + reasoning_effort:low.
#   KV is reserved by -c only — think ON does not change the reserved footprint.
#   Sample params per unsloth card (temp 0.7, top-p 0.80, top-k 20, min-p 0.0,
#   presence-penalty 1.5, repetition-penalty 1.0).
# kvarn4 K / kvarn3 V = house 128K+ pattern with tail 1024 on low-bit target cache.
# Source: beellama.cpp | https://huggingface.co/unsloth/qwen3.8-27B-gguf

. "$PSScriptRoot\beellama_common.ps1"

$modelKey = "Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q5_K_S"
if (-not (Test-Path $Model[$modelKey]))
{
  Write-Error "Missing $modelKey at $($Model[$modelKey])"
  exit 1
}

Write-Host "Launching: Qwen3.8-27B UD-Q5_K_S + MTP beellama (144k MAX, think ON low, vision OFF, kvarn KV)" -ForegroundColor Green
& (Get-ServerBinary -Build "beellama") `
  -m $Model[$modelKey] `
  --port $Config.server.port --host $Config.server.host `
  -np 1 `
  --kv-unified `
  --spec-type draft-mtp `
  --spec-draft-n-max 2 `
  -ngl all `
  --ctx-size 147456 `
  -b 2048 -ub 256 `
  --cache-type-k kvarn4 --cache-type-v kvarn3 `
  --kv-tail-tokens 1024 `
  --flash-attn on `
  --cache-ram 0 `
  --jinja `
  --load-mode mlock `
  --no-warmup `
  --no-host --metrics `
  --log-timestamps --log-prefix --log-colors off `
  --reasoning on `
  --chat-template-kwargs '{"preserve_thinking":true, "reasoning_effort":"low"}' `
  --temp 0.7 --top-p 0.80 --top-k 20 --min-p 0.0 `
  --presence-penalty 1.5 --repeat-penalty 1.0


// ==== Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q5_K_S-mtp-quality-think-beellama.ps1 ====
# 48K ctx, q8_0 K / q8_0 V, b2048/ub256, think ON, vision OFF, MTP (beellama) — MAX QUALITY
#
# Qwen3.8-27B UD-Q5_K_S (Unsloth Dynamic) — PURE QUALITY copy of the maxctx script.
# On disk: 17.38 GiB. This one spends the VRAM on cache precision, not window:
#   Maxctx:   144K kvarn4/kvarn3, ~21.8 GB steady (max-ctx / speed).
#   This file: 48K q8_0/q8_0. 17.38 + 3.4 + 0.5 = 21.3 GB steady.
# WHY 48K (not 64K): q8_0 KV is ~4x the bytes/token of kvarn. At this model size,
#   64K q8 = 22.3 GB steady — the same class that OOMs (AD-Q5_K-Q4_K at 22.2 GB steady
#   was the documented cliff). Shrink ctx BEFORE dropping q8: 48K keeps full q8 cache
#   fidelity inside the proven budget; 64K would force kvarn — the cut this file
#   exists to avoid.
# Hybrid Qwen3.8 is KV-sensitive; q8 is the quality floor. kvarn stays on maxctx.
# MTP head verified baked in (nextn tensors in GGUF header): --spec-type draft-mtp.
# think ON (low) + vision OFF: --reasoning on, preserve_thinking:true + reasoning_effort:low.
#   Sample params per unsloth card (temp 0.7, top-p 0.80, top-k 20, min-p 0.0,
#   presence-penalty 1.5, repetition-penalty 1.0).
# Sources: https://unsloth.ai/docs/models/qwen3.8 | https://github.com/sudoingX/qwen38-mtp

. "$PSScriptRoot\beellama_common.ps1"

$modelKey = "Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q5_K_S"
if (-not (Test-Path $Model[$modelKey]))
{
  Write-Error "Missing $modelKey at $($Model[$modelKey])"
  exit 1
}

Write-Host "Launching: Qwen3.8-27B UD-Q5_K_S + MTP beellama (48k QUALITY, think ON low, vision OFF, q8 KV)" -ForegroundColor Green
& (Get-ServerBinary -Build "beellama") `
  -m $Model[$modelKey] `
  --port $Config.server.port --host $Config.server.host `
  -np 1 `
  --kv-unified `
  --spec-type draft-mtp `
  --spec-draft-n-max 2 `
  -ngl all `
  --ctx-size 49152 `
  -b 2048 -ub 256 `
  --cache-type-k q8_0 --cache-type-v q8_0 `
  --flash-attn on `
  --cache-ram 0 `
  --jinja `
  --load-mode mlock `
  --no-warmup `
  --no-host --metrics `
  --log-timestamps --log-prefix --log-colors off `
  --reasoning on `
  --chat-template-kwargs '{"preserve_thinking":true, "reasoning_effort":"low"}' `
  --temp 0.7 --top-p 0.80 --top-k 20 --min-p 0.0 `
  --presence-penalty 1.5 --repeat-penalty 1.0


// ==== beellama_common.ps1 ====
# beellama_common.ps1

# Shared configuration for all beellama launch scripts.

# Reads config.json for base paths, exposes model/drafter/mmproj lookups

# and helper functions (Get-ServerBinary, Get-CommonFlags).

#

# Dot-source from any launch script in /run:

#   . "$PSScriptRoot\beellama_common.ps1"



$RepoRoot = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot

if (-not $RepoRoot)

{

    $RepoRoot = Split-Path -Parent (Split-Path -Parent $PSCommandPath)

}



# ---------- Load config.json ----------

$ConfigPath = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "config.json"

if (-not (Test-Path $ConfigPath))

{

    Write-Error "config.json not found at $ConfigPath"

    exit 1

}

$Config = Get-Content $ConfigPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json



# ---------- Resolve base paths ----------

$AltModelsPath    = "D:\.lmstudio\models"       # alternate drive (DeltaNet models, LuceBox drafts, jackrong models moved off C:)



$ModelBase_Ornith   = Join-Path $AltModelsPath "AtomicChat"

$ModelBase_Bartowski = Join-Path $AltModelsPath "bartowski"   # Bartowski quants (Ornith-1.5 Q4_K_L etc.)

$ModelBase_UnslothGlimmer = Join-Path $AltModelsPath "unsloth\meta-glimmer30b-gguf"  # Glimmer UD-Q4_K_XL model (draft+mmproj moved to meta-models/)

$ModelBase_GlimmerZLabs = Join-Path $ModelBase_Glimmer "z-labs"   # Glimmer DFlash drafter (Q4_K_M-tuned)

# Qwen3.8-27B (Unsloth GGUF, MTP head baked in — no separate draft model) — today's release, D:\ alt drive

$ModelBase_Qwen38    = Join-Path $AltModelsPath "unsloth\qwen3.8-27B-gguf"

# Qwen3.8-27B Atomic Dynamic (AD-) GGUFs — D:\.lmstudio\models\AtomicChat (flat)

$ModelBase_Qwen38Atomic = Join-Path $AltModelsPath "AtomicChat"

# Qwen3.8-27B DSPark drafter (DimInfer, Q8_0) — D:\.lmstudio\models\dimInfer

$ModelBase_DSpark = Join-Path $AltModelsPath "dimInfer"

# ---------- Model catalog ----------

# Target models keyed by friendly name

$Model = @{

    # Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B (AtomicChat IQ4_XS) — MoE, 40L, 256 exp/8 active +1 shared, qwen35moe, 262K ctx, vision

    #   Main file 18.73 GB; MTP head ships SEPARATELY as a draft GGUF (not downloadable yet).

    #   Vision projector: mmproj-Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-F16.gguf (not downloaded yet).

    "Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-IQ4_XS"  = Join-Path $ModelBase_Ornith "Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-IQ4_XS.gguf"

    # Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B (Bartowski Q4_K_L) — MoE, 41 blocks, 256 exp/8 active +1 shared, qwen35moe, 262K ctx

    #   Bartowski quant 22.24 GB (20.70 GiB on disk); higher fidelity than IQ4_XS at the cost of VRAM/ctx headroom.

    #   Full MoE offload via --n-cpu-moe needed for 131K+ ctx on 24 GB; see run/Ornith*Q4_K_L*.ps1 for budget.

    "Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-Q4_K_L"  = Join-Path $ModelBase_Bartowski "Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B-Q4_K_L.gguf"

    # Meta Muse Glimmer 30B — legacy KQuant path (D:\.lmstudio\models\meta-models, 15.61 GB) — kept as fallback

    # Dense 52L, 6656 hidden, 32Q/2KV, SWA 2048 [L,L,L,G] x13 = 39 local + 13 global, RoPE theta 500k, 131072 ctx, vocab 202k

    "Muse-Glimmer-30B-KQuant-17GB" = Join-Path $ModelBase_Glimmer "muse-glimmer-30B-kquant-17gb.gguf"

    # Unsloth Muse Glimmer 30B Dynamic 2.0 — D:\.lmstudio\models\unsloth\meta-glimmer30b-gguf (per https://unsloth.ai/docs/models/muse-glimmer)

    # UD-Q4_K_XL 15.88GB is the 17GB-class 24GB-VRAM target (recommended start); UD-Q3/Q2 are headroom variants

    "Muse-Glimmer-30B-UD-Q4_K_XL" = Join-Path $ModelBase_UnslothGlimmer "Muse-Glimmer-30B-UD-Q4_K_XL.gguf"

    "Muse-Glimmer-30B-UD-Q3_K_XL" = Join-Path $ModelBase_UnslothGlimmer "Muse-Glimmer-30B-UD-Q3_K_XL.gguf"

    "Muse-Glimmer-30B-UD-Q2_K_XL" = Join-Path $ModelBase_UnslothGlimmer "Muse-Glimmer-30B-UD-Q2_K_XL.gguf"

    # Qwen3.8-27B (Unsloth Dynamic V3 — hybrid thinking + vision, 256K native ctx, MTP head in GGUF)

    #   Updated 2026-08-19: Q4_K_M/Q4_K_S replaced by UD-Q4_K_M/UD-Q4_K_S; UD-Q4_K_XL removed.

    #   MTP head verified baked in (blk.64.nextn.*), so --spec-type draft-mtp works standalone.

    #   UD-Q4_K_M = 15.3 GiB (fidelity); UD-Q4_K_S = 14.3 GiB (big-window / vision budget).

    #   Standalone MTP module (mtp-Qwen3.8-27B-Q4_0.gguf) is redundant for quants above UD-Q2_K_XL.

    "Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q4_K_M"      = Join-Path $ModelBase_Qwen38 "Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q4_K_M.gguf"

    "Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q4_K_S"      = Join-Path $ModelBase_Qwen38 "Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q4_K_S.gguf"

    #   UD-Q5_K_S = 17.38 GiB (2026-08-20) — fidelity tier replacing the AtomicChat AD set.

    "Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q5_K_S"      = Join-Path $ModelBase_Qwen38 "Qwen3.8-27B-UD-Q5_K_S.gguf"

    "Qwen3.8-27B-MTP"            = Join-Path $ModelBase_Qwen38 "mtp-Qwen3.8-27B-Q4_0.gguf"

    # Atomic Dynamic — measured on disk (GiB), not HF decimal-GB labels

    #   AD-Q5_K-Q4_K = 17.27 GiB  (HF 18.6 GB) — mix, room for long kvarn ctx

    #   AD-Q5_K      = 18.84 GiB  (HF 20.2 GB) — quality; 128k will not fit this box

    "Qwen3.8-27B-AD-Q5_K-Q4_K"   = Join-Path $ModelBase_Qwen38Atomic "Qwen3.8-27B-AD-Q5_K-Q4_K.gguf"

    "Qwen3.8-27B-AD-Q5_K"        = Join-Path $ModelBase_Qwen38Atomic "Qwen3.8-27B-AD-Q5_K.gguf"

}



# DFlash draft models (Ardenzard GGUFs for beellama.cpp DFlash)

$Drafter = @{

    "Qwen38-BF16"   = Join-Path $ModelBase_Qwen38 "mmproj-BF16.gguf"

# ---------- Binary resolution ----------

# Picks the right llama-server.exe based on build type.

function Get-ServerBinary

{

    param(

        [ValidateSet("beellama_fork", "beellama", "beellama_prebuilt", "ik_llama", "llama.cpp")]

        [string]$Build = "beellama_fork"

    )



    $RelPath  = $Config.binaries.$Build

    $FullPath = Join-Path $RepoRoot $RelPath



    if (Test-Path $FullPath)

    {

        return $FullPath

    }



    Write-Error "Build '$Build' not found at: $FullPath"

    exit 1

}



# ---------- Common launch flags ----------

# Returns an array of CLI arguments for llama-server.

#

# SpecMode controls speculative decoding:

#   "dflash" — cross-attention draft (requires -DraftModel)

#   "mtp"    — multi-token prediction (auto-detected by fork; do NOT pass --spec-type)

#   "none"   — no speculative decoding

#

# MTP and DFlash are mutually exclusive per launch.

function Get-CommonFlags

{

    param(

        [ValidateSet("mtp", "dflash", "none")]

        [string]$SpecMode = "dflash",

        [string]$DraftModel = "",

        [string]$CtxSize = "122800",

        [string]$CacheK = "turbo4",

        [string]$CacheV = "turbo4",

        [string]$CrossCtx = "256",

        [string]$BatchSize = "256",

        [string]$UBatchSize = "64",

        [string]$MmprojPath = "",

        [switch]$Reasoning,

        [switch]$SkipMmproj

    )

    $Flags = @()



    # Multimodal projector

    if (-not $SkipMmproj -and $MmprojPath)

    {

        $Flags += "--mmproj", $MmprojPath

        $Flags += "--no-mmproj-offload"

    }



    # Speculative decoding

    if ($SpecMode -eq "dflash")

    {

        if (-not $DraftModel)

        {

            Write-Error "DFlash mode requires -DraftModel. Provide a DFlash draft GGUF path."

            exit 1

        }

        $Flags += "--spec-type",            "dflash"

        $Flags += "--spec-draft-model",     $DraftModel

        $Flags += "--spec-dflash-cross-ctx", $CrossCtx

        $Flags += "--spec-draft-ngl",       "all"

    } elseif ($SpecMode -eq "mtp")

    {

        # MTP: explicit spec-type required by main beellama.cpp (fork auto-detects it)

        $Flags += "--spec-type", "draft-mtp", "--spec-draft-n-max", "2"

    }

    # "none": no speculative decoding flags



    # Server + inference flags

    $Flags += @(

        "--port",           "$($Config.server.port)",

        "--host",           $Config.server.host,

        "-np",              "1",

        "--kv-unified",

        "-ngl",             "all",

        "--ctx-size",       $CtxSize,

        "-b",               $BatchSize,

        "-ub",              $UBatchSize,

        "--cache-type-k",   $CacheK,

        "--cache-type-v",   $CacheV,

        "--flash-attn",     "on",

        "--cache-ram",      "0",

        "--jinja",

        "--no-mmap",

        "--mlock",

        "--no-host",

        "--metrics",

        "--log-timestamps",

        "--log-prefix",

        "--log-colors",     "off",

        "--temp",           "0.6",

        "--top-k",          "20",

        "--min-p",          "0.0"

    )



    # Reasoning mode

    if ($Reasoning)

    {

        $Flags += "--reasoning",              "on"

        $Flags += "--chat-template-kwargs",   '{"preserve_thinking":true}'

    } else

    {

        $Flags += "--reasoning",              "off"

        $Flags += "--chat-template-kwargs",   '{"preserve_thinking":false}'

    }



    return $Flags

}



// ==== test-lucebox.ps1 ====
param($ScriptPath)

$proc = Start-Process powershell -ArgumentList "-File `"$ScriptPath`"" -PassThru -NoNewWindow -RedirectStandardOutput "$env:TEMP\lucebox-test-out.txt" -RedirectStandardError "$env:TEMP\lucebox-test-err.txt"

# Wait for "[server] listening" in output, up to 60s
$deadline = (Get-Date).AddSeconds(60)
$ready = $false
while ((Get-Date) -lt $deadline) {
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500
    if (Test-Path "$env:TEMP\lucebox-test-out.txt") {
        $content = Get-Content "$env:TEMP\lucebox-test-out.txt" -Raw
        if ($content -match '\[server\] listening') {
            $ready = $true
            break
        }
    }
    if ($proc.HasExited) {
        Write-Host "Process exited early (code $($proc.ExitCode))"
        Get-Content "$env:TEMP\lucebox-test-err.txt" -Tail 20
        Get-Content "$env:TEMP\lucebox-test-out.txt" -Tail 20
        exit 1
    }
}

if (-not $ready) {
    Write-Host "TIMEOUT: server didn't reach listening state"
    $proc.Kill()
    Get-Content "$env:TEMP\lucebox-test-err.txt" -Tail 20
    exit 1
}

Write-Host "Server listening!"
Write-Host "--- /v1/models ---"
try { Invoke-RestMethod -Uri "http://$($configHost):$($configPort)/v1/models" -TimeoutSec 5 } catch { Write-Host "ERROR: $_" }

Write-Host "--- tiny chat ---"
$body = @{model="localmodel"; messages=@(@{role="user"; content="hi"}; @{role="assistant"; content="hello"}); max_tokens=1; stream=$false} | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 4 -Compress
try { $r = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri "http://$($configHost):$($configPort)/v1/chat/completions" -Method Post -Body $body -ContentType "application/json" -TimeoutSec 30; Write-Host "OK: $($r.choices[0].message.content)" } catch { Write-Host "CHAT ERROR: $_" }

$proc.Kill()
Write-Host "Done."


// ==== query-ornith-models.ps1 ====
#Requires -Version 7
<#
.SYNOPSIS
    Queries HuggingFace for the latest Ornith models and their download links.

.DESCRIPTION
    Uses the HuggingFace API to search for models with "ornith" in the name,
    sorted by latest modification date. Outputs a formatted table with model
    details and direct GGUF download links.

    Sort order: "lastModified", "trending", "likes", "downloads", "createdAt" (default: "lastModified")

.PARAMETER Limit
    Maximum number of results to return (default: 20)

.PARAMETER Format
    Output format: "table", "json", "csv" (default: "table")
#>

[CmdletBinding()]
param(
    [ValidateSet("lastModified", "likes", "downloads", "createdAt")]
    [string]$SortBy = "lastModified",
    [int]$Limit = 20,

    [ValidateSet("table", "json", "csv")]
    [string]$Format = "table"
)

$baseUrl = "https://huggingface.co/api/models"

# Build URL with query parameters
$queryString = "search=ornith&sort=$SortBy&direction=-1&limit=$Limit"
$fullUrl = $baseUrl + "?" + $queryString

try {
    $response = Invoke-RestMethod -Uri $fullUrl -Method GET
} catch {
    Write-Error "Failed to query HuggingFace API: $_"
    exit 1
}

if ($response.Count -eq 0) {
    Write-Warning "No Ornith models found on HuggingFace."
    exit 0
}

$results = foreach ($model in $response) {
    $modelId = $model.id
    $downloads = $model.downloads
    $likes = $model.likes
    $modified = if ($model.lastModified) { [datetime]::Parse($model.lastModified) } else { $null }

    # Build download links for common GGUF variants
    $ggufUrl = "https://huggingface.co/$modelId/resolve/main/"

    [PSCustomObject]@{
        Model       = $modelId
        Format      = $model.pipeline_tag
        Tags        = ($model.tags -join ", ")
        Downloads   = $downloads
        Likes       = $likes
        Modified    = $modified
        HFPage      = "https://huggingface.co/$modelId"
        GGUFBase    = $ggufUrl
    }
}

switch ($Format) {
    "table" {
        $results | Format-Table -AutoSize -Property @(
            @{Label="Model"; Expression={$_.Model}},
            @{Label="Downloads"; Expression={$_.Downloads}},
            @{Label="Likes"; Expression={$_.Likes}},
            @{Label="Modified"; Expression={$_.Modified.ToString("yyyy-MM-dd")}}
        )
        Write-Host "`nFull details (HF page + GGUF base URL):" -ForegroundColor Cyan
        $results | Format-Table -AutoSize -Property @(
            "Model", "HFPage", "GGUFBase"
        )
    }
    "json" {
        $results | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 3
    }
    "csv" {
        $results | ConvertTo-Csv -NoTypeInformation
    }
}


// ==== find-msvc-openmp-runtime.ps1 ====
param(
    [ValidateSet("x64")]
    [string] $Architecture = "x64"
)

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

function Get-VersionOrZero($File) {
    try {
        return [Version] $File.VersionInfo.FileVersion
    } catch {
        return [Version] "0.0.0.0"
    }
}

$programFilesX86 = [Environment]::GetEnvironmentVariable("ProgramFiles(x86)")
$programRoots = @($env:ProgramFiles, $programFilesX86) | Where-Object { $_ }

$vsRoots = @()
$vswhereCandidates = foreach ($programRoot in $programRoots) {
    Join-Path $programRoot "Microsoft Visual Studio\Installer\vswhere.exe"
}
$vswhereCandidates = $vswhereCandidates |
    Where-Object { Test-Path -LiteralPath $_ } |
    Sort-Object -Unique

$vswhereArgSets = @(
    ,@("-all", "-products", "*", "-requires", "Microsoft.VisualStudio.Component.VC.Redist.14.Latest", "-property", "installationPath"),
    ,@("-all", "-products", "*", "-requires", "Microsoft.VisualStudio.Component.VC.Tools.x86.x64", "-property", "installationPath"),
    ,@("-all", "-products", "*", "-property", "installationPath")
)

foreach ($vswhere in $vswhereCandidates) {
    foreach ($argSet in $vswhereArgSets) {
        $vsRoots += & $vswhere @argSet 2>$null
    }
}

foreach ($programRoot in $programRoots) {
    $visualStudioRoot = Join-Path $programRoot "Microsoft Visual Studio"
    if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $visualStudioRoot)) {
        continue
    }

    foreach ($versionRoot in Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $visualStudioRoot -Directory -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue) {
        foreach ($editionRoot in Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $versionRoot.FullName -Directory -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue) {
            $vsRoots += $editionRoot.FullName
        }
    }
}

$vsRoots = $vsRoots |
    Where-Object { $_ -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $_) } |
    Sort-Object -Unique

$searchRoots = foreach ($vsRoot in $vsRoots) {
    foreach ($subdir in @("VC\Redist\MSVC", "VC\Tools\MSVC")) {
        $path = Join-Path $vsRoot $subdir
        if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $path) {
            $path
        }
    }
}
$searchRoots = $searchRoots | Sort-Object -Unique

$candidates = foreach ($searchRoot in $searchRoots) {
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $searchRoot -Recurse -File -Filter "libomp140.x86_64.dll" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
        Where-Object {
            $_.FullName -match "\\$Architecture\\" -and (
                $_.FullName -match "\\Microsoft\.VC\d+\.OpenMP\.LLVM\\" -or
                $_.FullName -match "\\bin\\Host(?:x86|x64)\\$Architecture\\"
            )
        }
}

$runtime = $candidates |
    Sort-Object `
        @{ Expression = { Get-VersionOrZero $_ } },
        @{ Expression = { $_.FullName } } `
        -Descending |
    Select-Object -First 1

if (-not $runtime) {
    $searched = if ($searchRoots) { $searchRoots -join "; " } else { "<none>" }
    throw "MSVC $Architecture libomp140.x86_64.dll not found. Searched: $searched"
}

$runtime.FullName

#!/usr/bin/env pwsh

# Basedir on device
$basedir=".\pkg-snapdragon"

    exit -1

    $cli_opts=$args[1..($args.Count - 1)]

}

$device="HTP0"

    $device=$env:D
}

if ($null -ne $env:V) {
    $env:GGML_HEXAGON_VERBOSE=$env:V

    $env:GGML_HEXAGON_NDEV=$env:NDEV
}

    $env:GGML_HEXAGON_HOSTBUF=$env:HB
}
